## 2026-04-07T04:21:14.777Z — Archives Consulted
Playbook:
- Company Overview: **No Man's AI** is a virtual office that runs business operations through role-based employees, shared archives, private desk notes, and a clear chain of command. Deliver reliable office work with visible collaboration, documented decisions, and minimal owner interruption. - **4 
- Customer Service Policy: - All client messages acknowledged within 1 business hour - Full responses within 4 business hours - If resolution requires more time, send status update with ETA
- Escalation Protocol: - Developer-to-developer code review disagreements - Minor scheduling conflicts - **Resolution**: Direct conversation between parties
- Financial Rules: | Amount | Approver | |--------|----------| | Any outward payment, refund, or credit | Rowan Pike final approval |

Shared knowledge:
- Active Projects: - **Client**: Meridian Corp - **Lead Dev**: Ava Kim (React A) - **Support**: Sam (React C)
- Client Directory: - **Contact**: Dana Whitfield, Product Director - **Email**: dana@meridiancorp.example - **Priority**: VIP
- Office Procedures: 1. Each employee starts at their assigned station 2. Check personal queue and task board 3. Collaborate as needed — walk to the relevant location
- Red Terminal Events: Priority: high From: Rowan Pike Ensure that any unresolved issues or escalations are properly handled.
